# L3 Phase 2 — Proposed Canonical Scenario Register

**Architecture Level:** L3 — Business Scenario Architecture
**Phase:** Phase 2 — Repository-Wide Scenario Consolidation
**Status:** Working artifact — Phase 2 Working IDs are NOT final L3 IDs
**Generated:** 2026-08-16
**Source:** Mechanical extraction of all 1,123 candidate rows from the five locked Phase 1 inventories, overlaid with evidence-backed dispositions from the eight Phase 2 Consolidation Watchlists
**Companion Report:** `generated/reviews/L3_Phase2_Repository_Wide_Consolidation_Review.md`

---

## How to Read This Register

* **Phase 2 Working ID** (`P2W-<SECTION>-<NNNN>`) is a working-session sequence number for this register only. It is NOT a final L3 scenario ID and must not be treated as one.
* **Phase 1 Candidate ID** is the authoritative traceability key carried forward unchanged from the locked Phase 1 inventories. It is never renumbered.
* **Disposition** is one of the eight Phase 2 values. The overwhelming majority of candidates carry `KEEP` because Sections A, F, G and H already underwent evidence-based fragmentation audits in Phase 1 (documented in their respective watchlists), and Sections B, C, D, E, I and J were confirmed as already right-sized registers. This Phase 2 pass did not re-open settled Phase 1 fragmentation judgments; it verified them and added the cross-section collision review that Phase 1 discovery could not perform (see the companion report Section 7 and 8).
* **BLOCKED** rows carry a specific watchlist issue ID and evidence citation. No blocked item was resolved by this pass. Where a blocked item's evidence referenced a Phase 1 candidate ID that has since been absorbed elsewhere (found during this pass — see the companion report), the reference was corrected to the surviving ID and the correction is noted in the Evidence column.
* No candidate was merged, split, or renumbered by this register. It is a review-and-classify pass, not a modification of the Phase 1 inventories.
